# Graph Report - himmel  (2026-11-21)

## Corpus Check
- 5646 files · ~10,865,864 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 1064 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .jsonl 242, .out 135, .patch 106)

## Summary
- 59414 nodes · 118935 edges · 3711 communities (2469 shown, 1242 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 97% EXTRACTED · 3% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 3460 edges (avg confidence: 0.78)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `6f88dc9e`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
